The Index mode is entered only on an explicit item-ID token, and is how Dispatch (the orchestrator) invokes it on items in "In Review" status: finds the associated PR, checks it strictly against the acceptance criteria (which it never… ([mike-bronner/workbench-dev-team](https://github.com/mike-bronner/workbench-dev-team/blob/HEAD/agents/holmes.md))
- **holmes-index**: Sherlock Holmes in The Index mode only — the item-ID token in, one App-signed verdict on the item's PR out. Dispatch workbench-dev-team:holmes, which routes the token here. ([mike-bronner/workbench-dev-team](https://github.com/mike-bronner/workbench-dev-team/blob/HEAD/agents/holmes-index.md))
- **holmes-lens**: Read-only helper that Sherlock Holmes dispatches inside his own review — a lens reviewer, a skeptic, a red-team attacker, a blue-team defender, or an auditor. It reads the tree it is pointed at, runs read-only commands and the existing tests that cover the change in that tree, and reports findings as its prompt asks. It never writes to the code under review, and never copies it. Dispatched only by Holmes, never by an orchestrator. ([mike-bronner/workbench-dev-team](https://github.com/mike-bronner/workbench-dev-team/blob/HEAD/agents/holmes-lens.md))
- **holmes-local**: Sherlock Holmes in Local mode only — a six-slot brief in, a prose verdict on the uncommitted working tree or the committed range (and PR number) its Workdir: names out, with no Index call and no GitHub write. Dispatch workbench-dev-team:holmes, which routes a brief here. ([mike-bronner/workbench-dev-team](https://github.com/mike-bronner/workbench-dev-team/blob/HEAD/agents/holmes-local.md))

- **humanizer-fidelity**: 의미 보존 감사 에이전트. gx-humanizer strict 모드에서 원문과 윤문본을 의미 단위로 대조하여 "내용이 보존됐는가"를 검증한다. 사실·주장·수치·고유명사·직접인용·인과관계·순서가 훼손됐는지 edit 단위로 탐지하여 pass/rollback을 판정한다. 한국어와 영어 양국어를 모두 검증한다. &lt;example&gt; User: strict 모드 윤문본 의미 보존 감사 요청 (원문 + 03_rewrite.md + diff) Agent: edit e012 [rollback] 수치 1.76조 누락 / edit e018 [rollback] "may"가 단정으로 전환(극성 훼손) / 나머지 31건 pass → audit_verdict: conditional_pass &lt;/example&gt; ([bs-koo/oh-my-gx](https://github.com/bs-koo/oh-my-gx/blob/HEAD/agents/humanizer-fidelity.md))
- **humanizer-naturalness**: 과윤문/잔존 검토 에이전트. gx-humanizer strict 모드에서 윤문본이 과도하게 문학적인지(과윤문), AI티가 잔존하는지, 리듬·장르를 이탈했는지 검토한다. 탐지 패턴(K/E/C)을 재계측해 잔존을 잡고, 과윤문 시그널을 탐지하여 accept / rewrite_round / rollback을 판정한다. 한국어와 영어 양국어를 모두 검토한다. &lt;example&gt; User: strict 모드 윤문본 자연스러움 검토 요청 (윤문본 + 02_detection.json) Agent: K1(문두 접속어) 잔존 1건, 과윤문 시그널 2개(문학화·구어화 과다) → verdict: rewrite_round / targets: [e007, e021] &lt;/example&gt; ([bs-koo/oh-my-gx](https://github.com/bs-koo/oh-my-gx/blob/HEAD/agents/humanizer-naturalness.md))
